- **how**: Explore the codebase and explain how a subsystem, a feature or a flow works, as the architecture and runtime flow a senior engineer needs before working in it, with the files and functions to look at. Use for 'how does X work', a code walkthrough before changing code you do not know, and placement, ownership and layering questions ('where should this live', 'which package owns this', 'is this the right layer').
